§4.1命令操作

①规范与代码分析

规范要点（xHCI 规范 §4.1 · p68）

要点 1 · 只有一条命令环，所有 xHC 命令都走它

There is only one Command Ring that is used for issuing xHC specific commands or commands related to Device Slots.
—— evidence/spec-41.txt L2（p68）

要点 2 · 发出三拍：TRB 上环 → 敲门铃寄存器 0（DB Target=0）→ xHC 执行

All xHC commands are issued by placing the desired Command TRB(s) (Section 6.4.3 Command TRBs) on the Command Ring, then ringing the xHC command Doorbell register, that is, writing the Host Controller Command code to the DB Target field of Doorbell register 0 (refer to Section 5.6 Doorbell Registers).
—— evidence/spec-41.txt L8-L10（p68）

要点 3 · 一切命令必产 Command Completion Event TRB 上事件环

All commands result in the generation of a Command Completion Event TRB (Section 6.4.3 Command TRBs) on the Event Ring.
—— evidence/spec-41.txt L19（p68）

要点 4 · CRCR（命令环控制寄存器）属 Operational 寄存器空间（§5.4.5）

The Command Ring Control Register is defined in the Operational Register space
—— evidence/spec-41.txt L3（p68）

4354static int queue_command(struct xhci_hcd *xhci, struct xhci_command *cmd,


4355			 u32 field1, u32 field2,


4356			 u32 field3, u32 field4, bool command_must_succeed)


4357{


4358	int reserved_trbs = xhci->cmd_ring_reserved_trbs;


4359	struct usb_hcd *hcd = xhci_to_hcd(xhci);


4360	int ret;


4361


4362	if ((xhci->xhc_state & XHCI_STATE_DYING) ||


4363		(xhci->xhc_state & XHCI_STATE_HALTED)) {


4364		xhci_dbg(xhci, "xHCI dying or halted, can't queue_command. state: 0x%x\n",


4365			 xhci->xhc_state);


4366		return -ESHUTDOWN;


4367	}


4368


4369	if (!HCD_HW_ACCESSIBLE(hcd)) {


4370		xhci_warn(xhci, "Can't queue command, xHC not accessible\n");


4371		return -ESHUTDOWN;


4372	}


4373


4374	if (!command_must_succeed)


4375		reserved_trbs++;


4376


4377	ret = prepare_ring(xhci, xhci->cmd_ring, EP_STATE_RUNNING,


4378			reserved_trbs, GFP_ATOMIC);


4379	if (ret < 0) {


4380		xhci_err(xhci, "ERR: No room for command on command ring\n");


4381		if (command_must_succeed)


4382			xhci_err(xhci, "ERR: Reserved TRB counting for "


4383					"unfailable commands failed.\n");


4384		return ret;


4385	}


4386


4387	cmd->command_trb = xhci->cmd_ring->enqueue;


4388


4389	/* if there are no other commands queued we start the timeout timer */


4390	if (list_empty(&xhci->cmd_list)) {


4391		xhci->current_cmd = cmd;


4392		xhci_mod_cmd_timer(xhci);


4393	}


4394


4395	list_add_tail(&cmd->cmd_list, &xhci->cmd_list);


4396


4397	queue_trb(xhci, xhci->cmd_ring, false, field1, field2, field3,


4398			field4 | xhci->cmd_ring->cycle_state);


4399	return 0;


4400}

2994static int xhci_handle_event_trb(struct xhci_hcd *xhci, struct xhci_interrupter *ir,


2995				 union xhci_trb *event)


2996{


2997	u32 trb_type;


2998


2999	trace_xhci_handle_event(ir->event_ring, &event->generic,


3000				xhci_trb_virt_to_dma(ir->event_ring->deq_seg,


3001						     ir->event_ring->dequeue));


3002


3003	/*


3004	 * Barrier between reading the TRB_CYCLE (valid) flag before, and any


3005	 * speculative reads of the event's flags/data below.


3006	 */


3007	rmb();


3008	trb_type = TRB_FIELD_TO_TYPE(le32_to_cpu(event->event_cmd.flags));


3009	/* FIXME: Handle more event types. */


3010


3011	switch (trb_type) {


3012	case TRB_COMPLETION:


3013		handle_cmd_completion(xhci, &event->event_cmd);


3014		break;


3015	case TRB_PORT_STATUS:


3016		handle_port_status(xhci, event);


3017		break;


3018	case TRB_TRANSFER:


3019		handle_tx_event(xhci, ir, &event->trans_event);


3020		break;


3021	case TRB_DEV_NOTE:


3022		handle_device_notification(xhci, event);


3023		break;


3024	default:


3025		if (trb_type >= TRB_VENDOR_DEFINED_LOW)


3026			handle_vendor_event(xhci, event, trb_type);


3027		else


3028			xhci_warn(xhci, "ERROR unknown event type %d\n", trb_type);


3029	}


3030	/* Any of the above functions may drop and re-acquire the lock, so check


3031	 * to make sure a watchdog timer didn't mark the host as non-responsive.


3032	 */


3033	if (xhci->xhc_state & XHCI_STATE_DYING) {


3034		xhci_dbg(xhci, "xHCI host dying, returning from event handler.\n");


3035		return -ENODEV;


3036	}


3037


3038	return 0;


3039}
drivers/usb/host/xhci-ring.c:1810-1948 · handle_cmd_completion · d9008e061539
1810static void handle_cmd_completion(struct xhci_hcd *xhci,


1811		struct xhci_event_cmd *event)


1812{


1813	unsigned int slot_id = TRB_TO_SLOT_ID(le32_to_cpu(event->flags));


1814	u32 status = le32_to_cpu(event->status);


1815	u64 cmd_dma;


1816	dma_addr_t cmd_dequeue_dma;


1817	u32 cmd_comp_code;


1818	union xhci_trb *cmd_trb;


1819	struct xhci_command *cmd;


1820	u32 cmd_type;


1821


1822	if (slot_id > xhci->max_slots) {


1823		xhci_warn(xhci, "Invalid slot_id %u\n", slot_id);


1824		return;


1825	}


1826


1827	cmd_dma = le64_to_cpu(event->cmd_trb);


1828	cmd_trb = xhci->cmd_ring->dequeue;


1829


1830	trace_xhci_handle_command(xhci->cmd_ring, &cmd_trb->generic, cmd_dma);


1831


1832	cmd_comp_code = GET_COMP_CODE(le32_to_cpu(event->status));


1833


1834	/* If CMD ring stopped we own the trbs between enqueue and dequeue */


1835	if (cmd_comp_code == COMP_COMMAND_RING_STOPPED) {


1836		complete_all(&xhci->cmd_ring_stop_completion);


1837		return;


1838	}


1839


1840	cmd_dequeue_dma = xhci_trb_virt_to_dma(xhci->cmd_ring->deq_seg,


1841			cmd_trb);


1842	/*


1843	 * Check whether the completion event is for our internal kept


1844	 * command.


1845	 */


1846	if (!cmd_dequeue_dma || cmd_dma != (u64)cmd_dequeue_dma) {


1847		xhci_warn(xhci,


1848			  "ERROR mismatched command completion event\n");


1849		return;


1850	}


1851


1852	cmd = list_first_entry(&xhci->cmd_list, struct xhci_command, cmd_list);


1853


1854	cancel_delayed_work(&xhci->cmd_timer);


1855


1856	if (cmd->command_trb != xhci->cmd_ring->dequeue) {


1857		xhci_err(xhci,


1858			 "Command completion event does not match command\n");


1859		return;


1860	}


1861


1862	/*


1863	 * Host aborted the command ring, check if the current command was


1864	 * supposed to be aborted, otherwise continue normally.


1865	 * The command ring is stopped now, but the xHC will issue a Command


1866	 * Ring Stopped event which will cause us to restart it.


1867	 */


1868	if (cmd_comp_code == COMP_COMMAND_ABORTED) {


1869		xhci->cmd_ring_state = CMD_RING_STATE_STOPPED;


1870		if (cmd->status == COMP_COMMAND_ABORTED) {


1871			if (xhci->current_cmd == cmd)


1872				xhci->current_cmd = NULL;


1873			goto event_handled;


1874		}


1875	}


1876


1877	cmd_type = TRB_FIELD_TO_TYPE(le32_to_cpu(cmd_trb->generic.field[3]));


1878	switch (cmd_type) {


1879	case TRB_ENABLE_SLOT:


1880		xhci_handle_cmd_enable_slot(slot_id, cmd, cmd_comp_code);


1881		break;


1882	case TRB_DISABLE_SLOT:


1883		xhci_handle_cmd_disable_slot(xhci, slot_id, cmd_comp_code);


1884		break;


1885	case TRB_CONFIG_EP:


1886		if (!cmd->completion)


1887			xhci_handle_cmd_config_ep(xhci, slot_id);


1888		break;


1889	case TRB_EVAL_CONTEXT:


1890		break;


1891	case TRB_ADDR_DEV:


1892		xhci_handle_cmd_addr_dev(xhci, slot_id);


1893		break;


1894	case TRB_STOP_RING:


1895		WARN_ON(slot_id != TRB_TO_SLOT_ID(


1896				le32_to_cpu(cmd_trb->generic.field[3])));


1897		if (!cmd->completion)


1898			xhci_handle_cmd_stop_ep(xhci, slot_id, cmd_trb,


1899						cmd_comp_code);


1900		break;


1901	case TRB_SET_DEQ:


1902		WARN_ON(slot_id != TRB_TO_SLOT_ID(


1903				le32_to_cpu(cmd_trb->generic.field[3])));


1904		xhci_handle_cmd_set_deq(xhci, slot_id, cmd_trb, cmd_comp_code);


1905		break;


1906	case TRB_CMD_NOOP:


1907		/* Is this an aborted command turned to NO-OP? */


1908		if (cmd->status == COMP_COMMAND_RING_STOPPED)


1909			cmd_comp_code = COMP_COMMAND_RING_STOPPED;


1910		break;


1911	case TRB_RESET_EP:


1912		WARN_ON(slot_id != TRB_TO_SLOT_ID(


1913				le32_to_cpu(cmd_trb->generic.field[3])));


1914		xhci_handle_cmd_reset_ep(xhci, slot_id, cmd_trb, cmd_comp_code);


1915		break;


1916	case TRB_RESET_DEV:


1917		/* SLOT_ID field in reset device cmd completion event TRB is 0.


1918		 * Use the SLOT_ID from the command TRB instead (xhci 4.6.11)


1919		 */


1920		slot_id = TRB_TO_SLOT_ID(


1921				le32_to_cpu(cmd_trb->generic.field[3]));


1922		xhci_handle_cmd_reset_dev(xhci, slot_id);


1923		break;


1924	case TRB_NEC_GET_FW:


1925		xhci_handle_cmd_nec_get_fw(xhci, event);


1926		break;


1927	case TRB_GET_BW:


1928		break;


1929	default:


1930		/* Skip over unknown commands on the event ring */


1931		xhci_info(xhci, "INFO unknown command type %d\n", cmd_type);


1932		break;


1933	}


1934


1935	/* restart timer if this wasn't the last command */


1936	if (!list_is_singular(&xhci->cmd_list)) {


1937		xhci->current_cmd = list_first_entry(&cmd->cmd_list,


1938						struct xhci_command, cmd_list);


1939		xhci_mod_cmd_timer(xhci);


1940	} else if (xhci->current_cmd == cmd) {


1941		xhci->current_cmd = NULL;


1942	}


1943


1944event_handled:


1945	xhci_complete_del_and_free_cmd(cmd, cmd_comp_code, COMP_PARAM(status));


1946


1947	inc_deq(xhci, xhci->cmd_ring);


1948}
drivers/usb/host/xhci-ring.c:1711-1722 · xhci_complete_del_and_free_cmd · d9008e061539
1711static void xhci_complete_del_and_free_cmd(struct xhci_command *cmd, u32 comp_code, u32 comp_param)


1712{


1713	list_del(&cmd->cmd_list);


1714


1715	if (cmd->completion) {


1716		cmd->status = comp_code;


1717		cmd->comp_param = comp_param;


1718		complete(cmd->completion);


1719	} else {


1720		kfree(cmd);


1721	}


1722}

上游 commit 576667bad341516edc4e18eb85acb0a2b4c9c9d9 · 2021-01-15 · Mathias Nyman（Intel） · drivers/usb/host/xhci-ring.c +2 行 · hunk @@ -2931,6 +2931,8 @@（queue_trb）

完整补丁 · git show 逐字

commit 576667bad341516edc4e18eb85acb0a2b4c9c9d9
Author: Mathias Nyman <mathias.nyman@linux.intel.com>
Date:   Fri Jan 15 18:19:06 2021 +0200

    xhci: make sure TRB is fully written before giving it to the controller
    
    Once the command ring doorbell is rung the xHC controller will parse all
    command TRBs on the command ring that have the cycle bit set properly.
    
    If the driver just started writing the next command TRB to the ring when
    hardware finished the previous TRB, then HW might fetch an incomplete TRB
    as long as its cycle bit set correctly.
    
    A command TRB is 16 bytes (128 bits) long.
    Driver writes the command TRB in four 32 bit chunks, with the chunk
    containing the cycle bit last. This does however not guarantee that
    chunks actually get written in that order.
    
    This was detected in stress testing when canceling URBs with several
    connected USB devices.
    Two consecutive "Set TR Dequeue pointer" commands got queued right
    after each other, and the second one was only partially written when
    the controller parsed it, causing the dequeue pointer to be set
    to bogus values. This was seen as error messages:
    
    "Mismatch between completed Set TR Deq Ptr command & xHCI internal state"
    
    Solution is to add a write memory barrier before writing the cycle bit.
    
    Cc: <stable@vger.kernel.org>
    Tested-by: Ross Zwisler <zwisler@google.com>
    Signed-off-by: Mathias Nyman <mathias.nyman@linux.intel.com>
    Link: https://lore.kernel.org/r/20210115161907.2875631-2-mathias.nyman@linux.intel.com
    Signed-off-by: Greg Kroah-Hartman <gregkh@linuxfoundation.org>

 	trb->field[0] = cpu_to_le32(field1);
 	trb->field[1] = cpu_to_le32(field2);
 	trb->field[2] = cpu_to_le32(field3);
+	/* make sure TRB is fully written before giving it to the controller */
+	wmb();
 	trb->field[3] = cpu_to_le32(field4);
 
 	trace_xhci_queue_trb(ring, trb);

背景（何时 / 何硬件 / 谁报）

2021 年 1 月，xHCI 子系统维护者 Mathias Nyman（Intel）合入这枚补丁。问题发现于压力测试：多个 USB 设备同时在线、反复取消 URB 的场景——两条「Set TR Dequeue Pointer」命令背靠背进入命令环，第二条命令 TRB 还没写完就被控制器取走解析。硬件本身没有缺陷，这是纯软件侧的内存序问题：门铃敲响后 xHC 会立即回来解析命令环上 cycle 位已就绪的命令 TRB（§4.1 p68 的发出节拍），而驱动「先写完 TRB、再敲门铃」这个顺序在写缓冲与多核环境下并没有任何机制保证。对应课程 4.1 的三拍：上环→敲门铃→xHC 取指执行，补丁保证第一拍真正先于第二拍对硬件生效。

要解决的问题（症状 + 机制）

症状：压力测试中反复出现 "Mismatch between completed Set TR Deq Ptr command & xHCI internal state" 告警，出环指针被设成 bogus 值。机制：命令 TRB 长 16 字节，驱动分四个 32 位分片写入，含 cycle bit 的分片安排在最后写——但这只是程序顺序，普通 store 不保证实际落盘次序；当前一条命令完成、硬件立刻回来取下一条时，可能读到只写了一半、而 cycle bit 恰好已置位的 TRB，于是照常执行。规范背景（§4.1 p68）：命令的发出流程是「把命令 TRB 放上命令环，然后敲命令门铃寄存器 0」——“放完才敲”的语义在 SMP 下必须由写屏障兑现，否则门铃会抢在 TRB 完整之前通知 xHC。

代码逻辑（补丁前 → 后行为对照）

补丁前：queue_trb() 依次写 field[0..3]，field[3]（含 cycle bit）程序上最后写但无屏障——硬件可能在 field[0..2] 尚未全局可见时就看到 cycle 位翻转，取走半颗 TRB。补丁后：在写 field[3] 之前插入 wmb() 写屏障并加注释行（+2 行），保证前三个分片先于 cycle 位翻转全局可见——“TRB 完整写好才交给控制器”由架构屏障兑现；此后命令完成事件（Command Completion Event，§4.1 p68）回执对应的总是一颗完整 TRB。与代码摘录（drafts/code-excerpts.json cmd 组 L4397 queue_trb(...) 入环行、L438 门铃行）对读，补丁改动恰好落在“写 TRB”与“敲门铃”两拍之间的写路径上；对映课程动画，节拍从此是“写四片 → wmb → cycle 翻转 → 门铃”，任何一环都不会被硬件抢先看到。

补丁分帧动画 · 四幕走读 → 背景现场 → 补丁前错误行为 → diff 逐行走读 → 补丁后行为（对照规范出处）

§4.2主机控制器初始化

①规范与代码分析

规范要点（xHCI 规范 §4.2 · p69-70）

要点 1 · 复位后等 USBSTS.CNR 清零，才能写任何 Operational/Runtime 寄存器

After Chip Hardware Reset6 wait until the Controller Not Ready (CNR) flag in the USBSTS is ‘0’ before writing any xHC Operational or Runtime registers.
—— evidence/spec-42.txt L18-L19（p69）

要点 2 · 各步顺序不强制，但必须在 USBCMD R/S=1 之前全部完成

Note: This text does not imply a specific order for the following operations, however these operations shall be completed before setting the USBCMD register Run/Stop (R/S) bit
—— evidence/spec-42.txt L21-L22（p69）

要点 3 · CONFIG.MaxSlotsEn 使能软件要用的设备槽

Program the Max Device Slots Enabled (MaxSlotsEn) field in the CONFIG register (5.4.7) to enable the device slots that system software is going to
—— evidence/spec-42.txt L25（p69）

要点 4 · CRCR=命令环首 TRB 地址（含 Cycle 位）

Define the Command Ring Dequeue Pointer by programming the Command Ring Control Register (Section 5.4.5 Command Ring Control Register (CRCR)) with a 64-bit address pointing to the starting address of the first TRB of the Command Ring.
—— evidence/spec-42.txt L34-L36（p69）

要点 5 · R/S=1 开机——xHC 从此开始接受门铃引用

Write the USBCMD (5.4.1) to turn the host controller ON via setting the Run/Stop (R/S) bit to ‘1’. This operation allows the xHC to begin accepting doorbell references.
—— evidence/spec-42.txt L115-L116（p70）

补充（见引文卡）：DCBAAP 指向 Device Context Base Address Array——evidence/spec-42.txt L29-32（p69）；写 ERSTBA 即启用事件环——evidence/spec-42.txt L96-98（p70）。

5431int xhci_gen_setup(struct usb_hcd *hcd, xhci_get_quirks_t get_quirks)


5432{


5433	struct xhci_hcd		*xhci;


5434	/*


5435	 * TODO: Check with DWC3 clients for sysdev according to


5436	 * quirks


5437	 */


5438	struct device		*dev = hcd->self.sysdev;


5439	int			retval;


5440	u32			hcs_params1;


5441	u32			hc_capbase;


5442


5443	/* Accept arbitrarily long scatter-gather lists */


5444	hcd->self.sg_tablesize = ~0;


5445


5446	/* support to build packet from discontinuous buffers */


5447	hcd->self.no_sg_constraint = 1;


5448


5449	/* XHCI controllers don't stop the ep queue on short packets :| */


5450	hcd->self.no_stop_on_short = 1;


5451


5452	xhci = hcd_to_xhci(hcd);


5453


5454	if (!usb_hcd_is_primary_hcd(hcd)) {


5455		xhci_hcd_init_usb3_data(xhci, hcd);


5456		return 0;


5457	}


5458


5459	mutex_init(&xhci->mutex);


5460	xhci->main_hcd = hcd;


5461	xhci->cap_regs = hcd->regs;


5462	hc_capbase = readl(&xhci->cap_regs->hc_capbase);


5463	if (hc_capbase == U32_MAX) {


5464		xhci_warn(xhci, "Host controller not accessible, removed?\n");


5465		return -ENODEV;


5466	}


5467	xhci->op_regs = hcd->regs + FIELD_GET(HC_LENGTH, hc_capbase);


5468


5469	xhci->run_regs = hcd->regs +


5470		(readl(&xhci->cap_regs->run_regs_off) & RTSOFF_MASK);


5471	/* Cache read-only capability registers */


5472	hcs_params1 = readl(&xhci->cap_regs->hcs_params1);


5473	xhci->hcs_params2 = readl(&xhci->cap_regs->hcs_params2);


5474	xhci->hcs_params3 = readl(&xhci->cap_regs->hcs_params3);


5475	xhci->hci_version = FIELD_GET(HC_VERSION, hc_capbase);


5476	xhci->hcc_params = readl(&xhci->cap_regs->hcc_params);


5477	if (xhci->hci_version > 0x100)


5478		xhci->hcc_params2 = readl(&xhci->cap_regs->hcc_params2);


5479


5480	xhci->dma_mask_bits = 64;


5481	xhci->max_slots = min(FIELD_GET(HCS_SLOTS_MASK, hcs_params1), MAX_HC_SLOTS);


5482	xhci->max_ports = min(FIELD_GET(HCS_MAX_PORTS, hcs_params1), MAX_HC_PORTS);


5483


5484	/* xhci-plat or xhci-pci might have set max_interrupters already */


5485	if (!xhci->max_interrupters)


5486		xhci->max_interrupters = min(FIELD_GET(HCS_MAX_INTRS, hcs_params1), MAX_HC_INTRS);


5487	else if (xhci->max_interrupters > FIELD_GET(HCS_MAX_INTRS, hcs_params1))


5488		xhci->max_interrupters = FIELD_GET(HCS_MAX_INTRS, hcs_params1);


5489


5490	xhci->quirks |= quirks;


5491


5492	if (get_quirks)


5493		get_quirks(dev, xhci);


5494


5495	/* In xhci controllers which follow xhci 1.0 spec gives a spurious


5496	 * success event after a short transfer. This quirk will ignore such


5497	 * spurious event.


5498	 */


5499	if (xhci->hci_version > 0x96)


5500		xhci->quirks |= XHCI_SPURIOUS_SUCCESS;


5501


5502	if (xhci->hci_version == 0x95 && link_quirk) {


5503		xhci_dbg(xhci, "QUIRK: Not clearing Link TRB chain bits");


5504		xhci->quirks |= XHCI_LINK_TRB_QUIRK;


5505	}


5506


5507	/* Make sure the HC is halted. */


5508	retval = xhci_halt(xhci);


5509	if (retval)


5510		return retval;


5511


5512	xhci_zero_64b_regs(xhci);


5513


5514	xhci_dbg(xhci, "Resetting HCD\n");


5515	/* Reset the internal HC memory state and registers. */


5516	retval = xhci_reset(xhci, XHCI_RESET_LONG_USEC);


5517	if (retval)


5518		return retval;


5519	xhci_dbg(xhci, "Reset complete\n");


5520


5521	/*


5522	 * On some xHCI controllers (e.g. R-Car SoCs), the AC64 bit (bit 0)


5523	 * of HCCPARAMS1 is set to 1. However, the xHCs don't support 64-bit


5524	 * address memory pointers actually. So, this driver clears the AC64


5525	 * bit of xhci->hcc_params to call dma_set_coherent_mask(dev,


5526	 * DMA_BIT_MASK(32)) in this xhci_gen_setup().


5527	 */


5528	if (xhci->quirks & XHCI_NO_64BIT_SUPPORT)


5529		xhci->hcc_params &= ~HCC_64BIT_ADDR;


5530


5531	/*


5532	 * Set dma_mask and coherent_dma_mask to 64-bits if xHC supports


5533	 * 64-bit addressing, unless a controller-specific quirk callback


5534	 * limits the usable address width.


5535	 */


5536	if ((xhci->hcc_params & HCC_64BIT_ADDR) &&


5537	    !dma_set_mask(dev, DMA_BIT_MASK(xhci->dma_mask_bits))) {


5538		xhci_dbg(xhci, "Enabling %u-bit DMA addresses.\n",


5539			 xhci->dma_mask_bits);


5540		dma_set_coherent_mask(dev, DMA_BIT_MASK(xhci->dma_mask_bits));


5541	} else {


5542		/*


5543		 * This is to avoid error in cases where a 32-bit USB


5544		 * controller is used on a 64-bit capable system.


5545		 */


5546		retval = dma_set_mask(dev, DMA_BIT_MASK(32));


5547		if (retval)


5548			return retval;


5549		xhci_dbg(xhci, "Enabling 32-bit DMA addresses.\n");


5550		dma_set_coherent_mask(dev, DMA_BIT_MASK(32));


5551	}


5552


5553	spin_lock_init(&xhci->lock);


5554	INIT_LIST_HEAD(&xhci->cmd_list);


5555	INIT_DELAYED_WORK(&xhci->cmd_timer, xhci_handle_command_timeout);


5556	init_completion(&xhci->cmd_ring_stop_completion);


5557	xhci_hcd_page_size(xhci);


5558


5559	/* Allocate xHCI data structures */


5560	retval = xhci_mem_init(xhci, GFP_KERNEL);


5561	if (retval)


5562		return retval;


5563


5564	/* Initialize HCD and host controller data structures */


5565	xhci_init(hcd);


5566


5567	if (xhci_hcd_is_usb3(hcd))


5568		xhci_hcd_init_usb3_data(xhci, hcd);


5569	else


5570		xhci_hcd_init_usb2_data(xhci, hcd);


5571


5572	xhci_info(xhci, "hcc params 0x%08x hci version 0x%x quirks 0x%016llx\n",


5573		  xhci->hcc_params, xhci->hci_version, xhci->quirks);


5574


5575	return 0;


5576}
drivers/usb/host/xhci.c:182-239 · xhci_reset · d9008e061539
182int xhci_reset(struct xhci_hcd *xhci, u64 timeout_us)


183{


184	u32 command;


185	u32 state;


186	int ret;


187


188	state = readl(&xhci->op_regs->status);


189


190	if (state == ~(u32)0) {


191		if (!(xhci->xhc_state & XHCI_STATE_DYING))


192			xhci_warn(xhci, "Host not accessible, reset failed.\n");


193		return -ENODEV;


194	}


195


196	if ((state & STS_HALT) == 0) {


197		xhci_warn(xhci, "Host controller not halted, aborting reset.\n");


198		return -EBUSY;


199	}


200


201	xhci_dbg_trace(xhci, trace_xhci_dbg_init, "// Reset the HC");


202	command = readl(&xhci->op_regs->command);


203	command |= CMD_RESET;


204	writel(command, &xhci->op_regs->command);


205


206	/* Existing Intel xHCI controllers require a delay of 1 mS,


207	 * after setting the CMD_RESET bit, and before accessing any


208	 * HC registers. This allows the HC to complete the


209	 * reset operation and be ready for HC register access.


210	 * Without this delay, the subsequent HC register access,


211	 * may result in a system hang very rarely.


212	 */


213	if (xhci->quirks & XHCI_INTEL_HOST)


214		udelay(1000);


215


216	ret = xhci_handshake(&xhci->op_regs->command, CMD_RESET, 0, timeout_us);


217	if (ret)


218		return ret;


219


220	if (xhci->quirks & XHCI_ASMEDIA_MODIFY_FLOWCONTROL)


221		usb_asmedia_modifyflowcontrol(to_pci_dev(xhci_to_hcd(xhci)->self.controller));


222


223	xhci_dbg_trace(xhci, trace_xhci_dbg_init,


224			 "Wait for controller to be ready for doorbell rings");


225	/*


226	 * xHCI cannot write to any doorbells or operational registers other


227	 * than status until the "Controller Not Ready" flag is cleared.


228	 */


229	ret = xhci_handshake(&xhci->op_regs->status, STS_CNR, 0, timeout_us);


230


231	xhci->usb2_rhub.bus_state.port_c_suspend = 0;


232	xhci->usb2_rhub.bus_state.suspended_ports = 0;


233	xhci->usb2_rhub.bus_state.resuming_ports = 0;


234	xhci->usb3_rhub.bus_state.port_c_suspend = 0;


235	xhci->usb3_rhub.bus_state.suspended_ports = 0;


236	xhci->usb3_rhub.bus_state.resuming_ports = 0;


237


238	return ret;


239}

490static void xhci_set_cmd_ring_deq(struct xhci_hcd *xhci)


491{


492	dma_addr_t deq_dma;


493	u64 crcr;


494


495	deq_dma = xhci_trb_virt_to_dma(xhci->cmd_ring->deq_seg, xhci->cmd_ring->dequeue);


496	deq_dma &= CMD_RING_PTR_MASK;


497


498	crcr = xhci_read_64(xhci, &xhci->op_regs->cmd_ring);

drivers/usb/host/xhci.c:534-575 · xhci_init · d9008e061539
534static void xhci_init(struct usb_hcd *hcd)


535{


536	struct xhci_hcd *xhci = hcd_to_xhci(hcd);


537


538	xhci_dbg_trace(xhci, trace_xhci_dbg_init, "Starting %s", __func__);


539


540	/* Set the Number of Device Slots Enabled to the maximum supported value */


541	xhci_enable_max_dev_slots(xhci);


542


543	/* Initialize the Command ring */


544	xhci_ring_init(xhci, xhci->cmd_ring);


545	/*


546	 * Reserve one command ring TRB for disabling LPM.


547	 * Since the USB core grabs the shared usb_bus bandwidth mutex before


548	 * disabling LPM, we only need to reserve one TRB for all devices.


549	 */


550	xhci->cmd_ring_reserved_trbs = 1;


551	/* Set the address in the Command Ring Control register */


552	xhci_set_cmd_ring_deq(xhci);


553


554	/* Set Device Context Base Address Array pointer */


555	xhci_write_64(xhci, xhci->dcbaa.dma, &xhci->op_regs->dcbaa_ptr);


556


557	/* Set Doorbell array pointer */


558	xhci_set_doorbell_ptr(xhci);


559


560	/* Set USB 3.0 device notifications for function remote wake */


561	xhci_set_dev_notifications(xhci);


562


563	/* Initialize the Primary interrupter */


564	xhci_ring_init(xhci, xhci->interrupters[0]->event_ring);


565	xhci_add_interrupter(xhci, 0);


566	xhci->interrupters[0]->isoc_bei_interval = AVOID_BEI_INTERVAL_MAX;


567


568	/* Initializing Compliance Mode Recovery Data If Needed */


569	if (xhci_compliance_mode_recovery_timer_quirk_check()) {


570		xhci->quirks |= XHCI_COMP_MODE_QUIRK;


571		compliance_mode_recovery_timer_init(xhci);


572	}


573


574	xhci_dbg_trace(xhci, trace_xhci_dbg_init, "Finished %s", __func__);


575}

2294xhci_alloc_interrupter(struct xhci_hcd *xhci, unsigned int segs, gfp_t flags)


2295{


2296	struct device *dev = xhci_to_hcd(xhci)->self.sysdev;


2297	struct xhci_interrupter *ir;


2298	unsigned int max_segs;


2299	int ret;


2300


2301	if (!segs)


2302		segs = ERST_DEFAULT_SEGS;


2303


2304	max_segs = BIT(FIELD_GET(HCS_ERST_MAX, xhci->hcs_params2));


2305	segs = min(segs, max_segs);


2306


2307	ir = kzalloc_node(sizeof(*ir), flags, dev_to_node(dev));


2308	if (!ir)


2309		return NULL;


2310


2311	ir->event_ring = xhci_ring_alloc(xhci, segs, TYPE_EVENT, 0, flags);


2312	if (!ir->event_ring) {


2313		xhci_warn(xhci, "Failed to allocate interrupter event ring\n");


2314		kfree(ir);


2315		return NULL;


2316	}


2317


2318	ret = xhci_alloc_erst(xhci, ir->event_ring, &ir->erst, flags);


2319	if (ret) {


2320		xhci_warn(xhci, "Failed to allocate interrupter erst\n");


2321		xhci_ring_free(xhci, ir->event_ring);


2322		kfree(ir);


2323		return NULL;


2324	}


2325


2326	return ir;


2327}

2329void xhci_add_interrupter(struct xhci_hcd *xhci, unsigned int intr_num)


2330{


2331	struct xhci_interrupter *ir;


2332	u64 erst_base;


2333	u32 erst_size;


2334


2335	ir = xhci->interrupters[intr_num];


2336	ir->intr_num = intr_num;


2337	ir->ir_set = &xhci->run_regs->ir_set[intr_num];

2341	erst_size &= ~ERST_SIZE_MASK;

2346	erst_base &= ~ERST_BASE_ADDRESS_MASK;

drivers/usb/host/xhci.c:627-678 · xhci_run · d9008e061539
627int xhci_run(struct usb_hcd *hcd)


628{


629	u64 temp_64;


630	int ret;


631	struct xhci_hcd *xhci = hcd_to_xhci(hcd);


632	struct xhci_interrupter *ir = xhci->interrupters[0];


633	/* Start the xHCI host controller running only after the USB 2.0 roothub


634	 * is setup.


635	 */


636


637	hcd->uses_new_polling = 1;


638	if (hcd->msi_enabled)


639		ir->ip_autoclear = true;


640


641	if (!usb_hcd_is_primary_hcd(hcd))


642		return xhci_run_finished(xhci);


643


644	xhci_dbg_trace(xhci, trace_xhci_dbg_init, "xhci_run");


645


646	temp_64 = xhci_read_64(xhci, &ir->ir_set->erst_dequeue);


647	temp_64 &= ERST_PTR_MASK;


648	xhci_dbg_trace(xhci, trace_xhci_dbg_init,


649			"ERST deq = 64'h%0lx", (long unsigned int) temp_64);


650


651	xhci_set_interrupter_moderation(ir, xhci->imod_interval);


652


653	if (xhci->quirks & XHCI_NEC_HOST) {


654		struct xhci_command *command;


655


656		command = xhci_alloc_command(xhci, false, GFP_KERNEL);


657		if (!command)


658			return -ENOMEM;


659


660		ret = xhci_queue_vendor_command(xhci, command, 0, 0, 0,


661				TRB_TYPE(TRB_NEC_GET_FW));


662		if (ret)


663			xhci_free_command(xhci, command);


664	}


665	xhci_dbg_trace(xhci, trace_xhci_dbg_init,


666			"Finished %s for main hcd", __func__);


667


668	xhci_create_dbc_dev(xhci);


669


670	xhci_debugfs_init(xhci);


671


672	if (xhci_has_one_roothub(xhci))


673		return xhci_run_finished(xhci);


674


675	set_bit(HCD_FLAG_DEFER_RH_REGISTER, &hcd->flags);


676


677	return 0;


678}
drivers/usb/host/xhci.c:579-613 · xhci_run_finished · d9008e061539
579static int xhci_run_finished(struct xhci_hcd *xhci)


580{


581	struct xhci_interrupter *ir = xhci->interrupters[0];


582	unsigned long	flags;


583	u32		temp;


584


585	/*


586	 * Enable interrupts before starting the host (xhci 4.2 and 5.5.2).


587	 * Protect the short window before host is running with a lock


588	 */


589	spin_lock_irqsave(&xhci->lock, flags);


590


591	xhci_dbg_trace(xhci, trace_xhci_dbg_init, "Enable interrupts");


592	temp = readl(&xhci->op_regs->command);


593	temp |= (CMD_EIE);


594	writel(temp, &xhci->op_regs->command);


595


596	xhci_dbg_trace(xhci, trace_xhci_dbg_init, "Enable primary interrupter");


597	xhci_enable_interrupter(ir);


598


599	if (xhci_start(xhci)) {


600		xhci_halt(xhci);


601		spin_unlock_irqrestore(&xhci->lock, flags);


602		return -ENODEV;


603	}


604


605	xhci->cmd_ring_state = CMD_RING_STATE_RUNNING;


606


607	if (xhci->quirks & XHCI_NEC_HOST)


608		xhci_ring_cmd_db(xhci);


609


610	spin_unlock_irqrestore(&xhci->lock, flags);


611


612	return 0;


613}
drivers/usb/host/xhci.c:145-173 · xhci_start · d9008e061539
145int xhci_start(struct xhci_hcd *xhci)


146{


147	u32 temp;


148	int ret;


149


150	temp = readl(&xhci->op_regs->command);


151	temp |= (CMD_RUN);


152	xhci_dbg_trace(xhci, trace_xhci_dbg_init, "// Turn on HC, cmd = 0x%x.",


153			temp);


154	writel(temp, &xhci->op_regs->command);


155


156	/*


157	 * Wait for the HCHalted Status bit to be 0 to indicate the host is


158	 * running.


159	 */


160	ret = xhci_handshake(&xhci->op_regs->status,


161			STS_HALT, 0, XHCI_MAX_HALT_USEC);


162	if (ret == -ETIMEDOUT)


163		xhci_err(xhci, "Host took too long to start, "


164				"waited %u microseconds.\n",


165				XHCI_MAX_HALT_USEC);


166	if (!ret) {


167		/* clear state flags. Including dying, halted or removing */


168		xhci->xhc_state = 0;


169		xhci->run_graceperiod = jiffies + msecs_to_jiffies(500);


170	}


171


172	return ret;


173}

完整补丁 · git show 逐字

commit 24d2a690fb83c40913c0b3f38151ae3997a5898f
Author: Henry Tseng <henrytseng@qnap.com>
Date:   Fri Oct 9 18:16:14 2026 +0300

    usb: xhci: return an error if the host is not halted
    
    xhci_reset() returns 0 when the host is not halted, without ever writing
    CMD_RESET. Every other path that fails to reset the host returns an
    error, so callers that check the return value are told the reset
    succeeded on the one path where it did not happen.
    
    Return -EBUSY when the reset is aborted because the host is not halted.
    
    Signed-off-by: Henry Tseng <henrytseng@qnap.com>
    Signed-off-by: Mathias Nyman <mathias.nyman@linux.intel.com>
    Link: https://patch.msgid.link/20261009151624.618967-2-mathias.nyman@linux.intel.com
    Signed-off-by: Greg Kroah-Hartman <gregkh@linuxfoundation.org>

 		xhci_warn(xhci, "Host controller not halted, aborting reset.\n");

背景（何时 / 何硬件 / 谁报）

2026 年 10 月，QNAP（NAS 存储厂商）的 Henry Tseng 报告并提交：xhci_reset() 在主机控制器未 halted（未停机）时直接中止复位路径，却不写 CMD_RESET、也不返回错误。触发场景是主机未停机就有路径对控制器调用复位（驱动探测/恢复流程）。硬件无关——这是纯软件状态机的返回值语义缺陷。对应课程 4.2 的 halt→reset 握手节拍：复位的前提（主机已停机）与完成（CNR 清零）都要靠状态位逐级确认，前提不满足时必须把失败如实上抛，而不是伪装成功。

要解决的问题（症状 + 机制）

症状：调用方检查 xhci_reset() 返回值时被告知「复位成功」，而复位实际上根本没有执行——这是全部复位失败路径里唯一一个「失败却返回 0」的分支。机制：xhci_reset() 开头读 USBSTS 状态，若 (state & STS_HALT) == 0（主机未 halted），按语义复位应当中止；补丁前这个分支 return 0，与后续「写 CMD_RESET → handshake 等 CNR 清零 → 失败返回超时错误」的其他路径语义不一致。规范背景（§4.2 p69）：复位不是瞬时动作，而是带握手的流程——芯片硬件复位后要等 USBSTS.CNR 清零才允许写其它 Operational/Runtime 寄存器；前提不满足就应报错，让调用链不至于在「复位已完成」的假象上继续初始化。

代码逻辑（补丁前 → 后行为对照）

单行改动（drivers/usb/host/xhci.c，xhci_reset）：未 halted 中止分支 return 0 → return -EBUSY。补丁前：中止复位被当成成功，调用方在错误状态下继续走初始化。补丁后：-EBUSY 把「主机忙、未停机」如实上抛，调用方走错误处理——xhci_reset 的所有失败出口（未 halted / handshake 超时）语义一致。与代码摘录（drafts/code-excerpts.json init 组 L229，同函数内 xhci_handshake(&xhci->op_regs->status, STS_CNR, 0, timeout_us)）对读：那条握手失败会返回超时错误，本补丁让“没进入复位”同样返回错误；凡依赖返回值判断复位成败的调用方（探测、休眠恢复、错误恢复路径）从此拿到一致语义，验收时可直接对 -EBUSY 分支验证未停机场景。

补丁分帧动画 · 四幕走读 → 背景现场 → 补丁前错误行为 → diff 逐行走读 → 补丁后行为（对照规范出处）

§4.3USB 设备初始化

①规范与代码分析

规范要点（xHCI 规范 §4.3 · p71-80）

要点 1 · FS 设备先经 EP0 读 Device Descriptor 前 8 字节取 bMaxPacketSize0

For FS devices, system software should initially read the first 8 bytes of the USB Device Descriptor to retrieve the value of the bMaxPacketSize0 field and determine the actual Max Packet Size for the Default Control Endpoint, by issuing a USB GET_DESCRIPTOR request to the device, update the Default Control Endpoint Context with the actual Max Packet Size and inform the xHC of the context change. Step a describes this operation.
—— evidence/spec-43.txt L103-L107（p72）

要点 2 · 控制传输三段顺序：Setup → Data → Status（shall 要求）

The USB GET_DESCRIPTOR request requires a Data Stage, so the Setup Stage TD shall be followed by a Data Stage TD, then a Status Stage TD.
—— evidence/spec-43.txt L115-L116（p73）

要点 3 · Setup Stage TRB 字段：TRT=IN Data Stage(3)/Length=8/IOC=0/IDT=1/bmRequestType=80h/bRequest=6/wValue=0100h/wLength=8

Initialize the Setup Stage TD (a single Setup Stage TRB) on the Endpoint 0 Transfer Ring. • TRB Type = Setup Stage TRB. • Transfer Type (TRT) = IN Data Stage (3). • TRB Transfer Length = 8. • Interrupt On Completion (IOC) = 0. • Immediate Data (IDT) = 1.
—— evidence/spec-43.txt L120-L125（p73）

要点 4 · 三段就绪后敲设备槽门铃：DB Target = Control EP 0 Enqueue Pointer Update

Ring the Device Slots’ Doorbell with DB Target = Control EP 0 Enqueue Pointer Update.
—— evidence/spec-43.txt L181（p74）

要点 5 · Address Device 命令完成后才能对该槽发更多命令（等待语义）

System software shall wait for Address Device Command completion event on the Event Ring before issuing any more commands to the slot.
—— evidence/spec-43.txt L346（p76）

补充（见引文卡）：Data/Status Stage TRB 字段——evidence/spec-43.txt L136-154 / L156-173（p73）；Address Device 命令指向 Input Context——evidence/spec-43.txt L331-334（p76）。

drivers/usb/core/message.c:150-176 · usb_control_msg · d9008e061539
150int usb_control_msg(struct usb_device *dev, unsigned int pipe, __u8 request,


151		    __u8 requesttype, __u16 value, __u16 index, void *data,


152		    __u16 size, int timeout)


153{


154	struct usb_ctrlrequest *dr;


155	int ret;


156


157	dr = kmalloc_obj(struct usb_ctrlrequest, GFP_NOIO);


158	if (!dr)


159		return -ENOMEM;


160


161	dr->bRequestType = requesttype;


162	dr->bRequest = request;


163	dr->wValue = cpu_to_le16(value);


164	dr->wIndex = cpu_to_le16(index);


165	dr->wLength = cpu_to_le16(size);


166


167	ret = usb_internal_control_msg(dev, pipe, dr, data, size, timeout);


168


169	/* Linger a bit, prior to the next control message. */


170	if (dev->quirks & USB_QUIRK_DELAY_CTRL_MSG)


171		msleep(200);


172


173	kfree(dr);


174


175	return ret;


176}

1620static int xhci_urb_enqueue(struct usb_hcd *hcd, struct urb *urb, gfp_t mem_flags)


1621{


1622	struct xhci_hcd *xhci = hcd_to_xhci(hcd);


1623	unsigned long flags;


1624	int ret = 0;


1625	unsigned int slot_id, ep_index;


1626	unsigned int *ep_state;


1627	struct urb_priv	*urb_priv;


1628	int num_tds;


1629


1630	ep_index = xhci_get_endpoint_index(&urb->ep->desc);


1631


1632	if (usb_endpoint_xfer_isoc(&urb->ep->desc))


1633		num_tds = urb->number_of_packets;


1634	else if (usb_endpoint_is_bulk_out(&urb->ep->desc) &&


1635	    urb->transfer_buffer_length > 0 &&


1636	    urb->transfer_flags & URB_ZERO_PACKET &&


1637	    !(urb->transfer_buffer_length % usb_endpoint_maxp(&urb->ep->desc)))


1638		num_tds = 2;


1639	else


1640		num_tds = 1;


1641


1642	urb_priv = kzalloc_flex(*urb_priv, td, num_tds, mem_flags);


1643	if (!urb_priv)


1644		return -ENOMEM;


1645


1646	urb_priv->num_tds = num_tds;


1647	urb_priv->num_tds_done = 0;


1648	urb->hcpriv = urb_priv;


1649


1650	trace_xhci_urb_enqueue(urb);


1651


1652	spin_lock_irqsave(&xhci->lock, flags);


1653


1654	ret = xhci_check_args(hcd, urb->dev, urb->ep,


1655			      true, true, __func__);


1656	if (ret <= 0) {


1657		ret = ret ? ret : -EINVAL;


1658		goto free_priv;


1659	}


1660


1661	slot_id = urb->dev->slot_id;


1662


1663	if (!HCD_HW_ACCESSIBLE(hcd)) {


1664		ret = -ESHUTDOWN;


1665		goto free_priv;


1666	}


1667


1668	if (xhci->devs[slot_id]->rhub_port->link_inactive) {


1669		xhci_dbg(xhci, "Can't queue urb, port error, link inactive\n");


1670		ret = -ENODEV;


1671		goto free_priv;


1672	}


1673


1674	if (xhci->xhc_state & XHCI_STATE_DYING) {


1675		xhci_dbg(xhci, "Ep 0x%x: URB %p submitted for non-responsive xHCI host.\n",


1676			 urb->ep->desc.bEndpointAddress, urb);


1677		ret = -ESHUTDOWN;


1678		goto free_priv;


1679	}


1680


1681	ep_state = &xhci->devs[slot_id]->eps[ep_index].ep_state;


1682


1683	if (*ep_state & (EP_GETTING_STREAMS | EP_GETTING_NO_STREAMS)) {


1684		xhci_warn(xhci, "WARN: Can't enqueue URB, ep in streams transition state %x\n",


1685			  *ep_state);


1686		ret = -EINVAL;


1687		goto free_priv;


1688	}


1689	if (*ep_state & EP_SOFT_CLEAR_TOGGLE) {


1690		xhci_warn(xhci, "Can't enqueue URB while manually clearing toggle\n");


1691		ret = -EINVAL;


1692		goto free_priv;


1693	}


1694


1695	switch (usb_endpoint_type(&urb->ep->desc)) {


1696


1697	case USB_ENDPOINT_XFER_CONTROL:


1698		ret = xhci_queue_ctrl_tx(xhci, GFP_ATOMIC, urb,


1699					 slot_id, ep_index);


1700		break;


1701	case USB_ENDPOINT_XFER_BULK:


1702		ret = xhci_queue_bulk_tx(xhci, GFP_ATOMIC, urb,


1703					 slot_id, ep_index);


1704		break;


1705	case USB_ENDPOINT_XFER_INT:


1706		ret = xhci_queue_intr_tx(xhci, GFP_ATOMIC, urb,


1707				slot_id, ep_index);


1708		break;


1709	case USB_ENDPOINT_XFER_ISOC:


1710		ret = xhci_queue_isoc_tx_prepare(xhci, GFP_ATOMIC, urb,


1711				slot_id, ep_index);


1712	}


1713


1714	if (ret) {


1715free_priv:


1716		xhci_urb_free_priv(urb_priv);


1717		urb->hcpriv = NULL;


1718	}


1719	spin_unlock_irqrestore(&xhci->lock, flags);


1720	return ret;


1721}
drivers/usb/host/xhci-ring.c:3774-3919 · xhci_queue_ctrl_tx · d9008e061539
3774int xhci_queue_ctrl_tx(struct xhci_hcd *xhci, gfp_t mem_flags,


3775		struct urb *urb, int slot_id, unsigned int ep_index)


3776{


3777	struct xhci_ring *ep_ring;


3778	int num_trbs;


3779	int ret;


3780	struct usb_ctrlrequest *setup;


3781	struct xhci_generic_trb *start_trb;


3782	int start_cycle;


3783	u32 field;


3784	struct urb_priv *urb_priv;


3785	struct xhci_td *td;


3786


3787	ep_ring = xhci_urb_to_transfer_ring(xhci, urb);


3788	if (!ep_ring)


3789		return -EINVAL;


3790


3791	/*


3792	 * Need to copy setup packet into setup TRB, so we can't use the setup


3793	 * DMA address.


3794	 */


3795	if (!urb->setup_packet)


3796		return -EINVAL;


3797


3798	if ((xhci->quirks & XHCI_ETRON_HOST) &&


3799	    urb->dev->speed >= USB_SPEED_SUPER) {


3800		/*


3801		 * If next available TRB is the Link TRB in the ring segment then


3802		 * enqueue a No Op TRB, this can prevent the Setup and Data Stage


3803		 * TRB to be broken by the Link TRB.


3804		 */


3805		if (last_trb_on_seg(ep_ring->enq_seg, ep_ring->enqueue + 1)) {


3806			field = TRB_TYPE(TRB_TR_NOOP) | ep_ring->cycle_state;


3807			queue_trb(xhci, ep_ring, false, 0, 0,


3808					TRB_INTR_TARGET(0), field);


3809		}


3810	}


3811


3812	/* 1 TRB for setup, 1 for status */


3813	num_trbs = 2;


3814	/*


3815	 * Don't need to check if we need additional event data and normal TRBs,


3816	 * since data in control transfers will never get bigger than 16MB


3817	 * XXX: can we get a buffer that crosses 64KB boundaries?


3818	 */


3819	if (urb->transfer_buffer_length > 0)


3820		num_trbs++;


3821	ret = prepare_transfer(xhci, xhci->devs[slot_id],


3822			ep_index, urb->stream_id,


3823			num_trbs, urb, 0, mem_flags);


3824	if (ret < 0)


3825		return ret;


3826


3827	urb_priv = urb->hcpriv;


3828	td = &urb_priv->td[0];


3829


3830	/*


3831	 * Don't give the first TRB to the hardware (by toggling the cycle bit)


3832	 * until we've finished creating all the other TRBs.  The ring's cycle


3833	 * state may change as we enqueue the other TRBs, so save it too.


3834	 */


3835	start_trb = &ep_ring->enqueue->generic;


3836	start_cycle = ep_ring->cycle_state;


3837


3838	/* Queue setup TRB - see section 6.4.1.2.1 */


3839	/* FIXME better way to translate setup_packet into two u32 fields? */


3840	setup = (struct usb_ctrlrequest *) urb->setup_packet;


3841	field = 0;


3842	field |= TRB_IDT | TRB_TYPE(TRB_SETUP);


3843	if (start_cycle == 0)


3844		field |= 0x1;


3845


3846	/* xHCI 1.0/1.1 6.4.1.2.1: Transfer Type field */


3847	if ((xhci->hci_version >= 0x100) || (xhci->quirks & XHCI_MTK_HOST)) {


3848		if (urb->transfer_buffer_length > 0) {


3849			if (setup->bRequestType & USB_DIR_IN)


3850				field |= TRB_TX_TYPE(TRB_DATA_IN);


3851			else


3852				field |= TRB_TX_TYPE(TRB_DATA_OUT);


3853		}


3854	}


3855


3856	queue_trb(xhci, ep_ring, true,


3857		  setup->bRequestType | setup->bRequest << 8 | le16_to_cpu(setup->wValue) << 16,


3858		  le16_to_cpu(setup->wIndex) | le16_to_cpu(setup->wLength) << 16,


3859		  TRB_LEN(8) | TRB_INTR_TARGET(0),


3860		  /* Immediate data in pointer */


3861		  field);


3862


3863	/* If there's data, queue data TRBs */


3864	/* Only set interrupt on short packet for IN endpoints */


3865	if (usb_urb_dir_in(urb))


3866		field = TRB_ISP | TRB_TYPE(TRB_DATA);


3867	else


3868		field = TRB_TYPE(TRB_DATA);


3869


3870	if (urb->transfer_buffer_length > 0) {


3871		u32 length_field, remainder;


3872		u64 addr;


3873


3874		if (xhci_urb_suitable_for_idt(urb)) {


3875			memcpy(&addr, urb->transfer_buffer,


3876			       urb->transfer_buffer_length);


3877			le64_to_cpus(&addr);


3878			field |= TRB_IDT;


3879		} else {


3880			addr = (u64) urb->transfer_dma;


3881		}


3882


3883		remainder = xhci_td_remainder(xhci, 0,


3884				urb->transfer_buffer_length,


3885				urb->transfer_buffer_length,


3886				urb, 1);


3887		length_field = TRB_LEN(urb->transfer_buffer_length) |


3888				TRB_TD_SIZE(remainder) |


3889				TRB_INTR_TARGET(0);


3890		if (setup->bRequestType & USB_DIR_IN)


3891			field |= TRB_DIR_IN;


3892		queue_trb(xhci, ep_ring, true,


3893				lower_32_bits(addr),


3894				upper_32_bits(addr),


3895				length_field,


3896				field | ep_ring->cycle_state);


3897	}


3898


3899	/* Save the DMA address of the last TRB in the TD */


3900	td->end_trb = ep_ring->enqueue;


3901	td->end_seg = ep_ring->enq_seg;


3902


3903	/* Queue status TRB - see Table 7 and sections 4.11.2.2 and 6.4.1.2.3 */


3904	/* If the device sent data, the status stage is an OUT transfer */


3905	if (urb->transfer_buffer_length > 0 && setup->bRequestType & USB_DIR_IN)


3906		field = 0;


3907	else


3908		field = TRB_DIR_IN;


3909	queue_trb(xhci, ep_ring, false,


3910			0,


3911			0,


3912			TRB_INTR_TARGET(0),


3913			/* Event on completion */


3914			field | TRB_IOC | TRB_TYPE(TRB_STATUS) | ep_ring->cycle_state);


3915


3916	giveback_first_trb(xhci, slot_id, ep_index, 0,


3917			start_cycle, start_trb);


3918	return 0;


3919}
drivers/usb/host/xhci-ring.c:558-582 · xhci_ring_ep_doorbell · d9008e061539
558void xhci_ring_ep_doorbell(struct xhci_hcd *xhci,


559		unsigned int slot_id,


560		unsigned int ep_index,


561		unsigned int stream_id)


562{


563	__le32 __iomem *db_addr = &xhci->dba->doorbell[slot_id];


564	struct xhci_virt_ep *ep = &xhci->devs[slot_id]->eps[ep_index];


565	unsigned int ep_state = ep->ep_state;


566


567	/* Don't ring the doorbell for this endpoint if there are pending


568	 * cancellations because we don't want to interrupt processing.


569	 * We don't want to restart any stream rings if there's a set dequeue


570	 * pointer command pending because the device can choose to start any


571	 * stream once the endpoint is on the HW schedule.


572	 */


573	if (ep_state & (EP_STOP_CMD_PENDING | SET_DEQ_PENDING | EP_HALTED |


574			EP_CLEARING_TT | EP_DROP_PENDING))


575		return;


576


577	trace_xhci_ring_ep_doorbell(slot_id, DB_VALUE(ep_index, stream_id));


578


579	writel(DB_VALUE(ep_index, stream_id), db_addr);


580	/* flush the write */


581	readl(db_addr);


582}

commit f72b9aa821a2bfe4b6dfec4be19f264d0673b008
Author: Jay Chen <shawn2000100@gmail.com>
Date:   Thu Jul 17 10:31:07 2025 +0300

    usb: xhci: Set avg_trb_len = 8 for EP0 during Address Device Command
    
    There is a subtle contradiction between sections of the xHCI 1.2 spec
    regarding the initialization of Input Endpoint Context fields. Section
    4.8.2 ("Endpoint Context Initialization") states that all fields should
    be initialized to 0. However, Section 6.2.3 ("Endpoint Context", p.453)
    specifies that the Average TRB Length (avg_trb_len) field shall be
    greater than 0, and explicitly notes (p.454): "Software shall set
    Average TRB Length to '8' for control endpoints."
    
    Strictly setting all fields to 0 during initialization conflicts with
    the specific recommendation for control endpoints. In practice, setting
    avg_trb_len = 0 is not meaningful for the hardware/firmware, as the
    value is used for bandwidth calculation.
    
    Motivation: Our company is developing a custom Virtual xHC hardware
    platform that strictly follows the xHCI spec and its recommendations.
    During validation, we observed that enumeration fails and a parameter
    error (TRB Completion Code = 5) is reported if avg_trb_len for EP0 is
    not set to 8 as recommended by Section 6.2.3. This demonstrates the
    importance of assigning a meaningful, non-zero value to avg_trb_len,
    even in virtualized or emulated environments.
    
    This patch explicitly sets avg_trb_len to 8 for EP0 in
    xhci_setup_addressable_virt_dev(), as recommended in Section 6.2.3, to
    prevent potential issues with xHCI host controllers that enforce the
    spec strictly.
    
    Link: https://bugzilla.kernel.org/show_bug.cgi?id=220033
    Signed-off-by: Jay Chen <shawn2000100@gmail.com>
    Signed-off-by: Mathias Nyman <mathias.nyman@linux.intel.com>
    Link: https://lore.kernel.org/r/20250717073107.488599-4-mathias.nyman@linux.intel.com
    Signed-off-by: Greg Kroah-Hartman <gregkh@linuxfoundation.org>

背景（何时 / 何硬件 / 谁报）

2025 年 7 月，Jay Chen 报告：其公司正在开发一个严格遵循 xHCI 规范的自研虚拟 xHC 硬件平台，验证阶段设备枚举失败——Address Device 命令回 TRB Completion Code = 5（Parameter Error），bug 单在 kernel bugzilla 220033。根因不在虚拟平台自身，而是 Linux 驱动对规范两处条文的取舍：初始化输入端点上下文时全字段清零，其中 Average TRB Length（avg_trb_len）= 0，而严格按规范实现的控制器会判定该参数非法。主流真实硬件恰好不校验这个字段，问题因此在真机上从未暴露——虚拟平台反而成了规范的忠实裁判。对应课程 4.3 的 Address Device 输入上下文节拍。

要解决的问题（症状 + 机制）

症状：严格按规范实现的（虚拟）xHC 在 EP0 编址时返回 Parameter Error（完成码 5），设备枚举失败。机制：规范 §4.8.2（Endpoint Context Initialization）要求输入端点上下文所有字段初始化为 0；§6.2.3（Endpoint Context）却规定 Average TRB Length 应大于 0，且明确「Software shall set Average TRB Length to 8 for control endpoints」——两条条文在全 0 初始化面前互相矛盾。avg_trb_len 用于带宽计算，对控制端点填 0 没有意义。驱动此前按 §4.8.2 字面义把 EP0 的 avg_trb_len 留成 0：宽容的硬件忽略它，严格的硬件判错。课程侧对应证据：§4.3.3（p76）「Allocate an Input Context data structure (6.2.5) and initialize all fields to 0」之后逐字段初始化 EP0 Context——补丁即在这条全 0 路径上为控制端点开例外。

代码逻辑（补丁前 → 后行为对照）

+2 行（drivers/usb/host/xhci-mem.c，xhci_setup_addressable_virt_dev()）：在 ep0_ctx->deq 赋值之后、trace 之前插入 ep0_ctx->tx_info = cpu_to_le32(EP_AVG_TRB_LENGTH(8));（含一个空行）。补丁前：tx_info 整体为 0（含 avg_trb_len），严格控制器判 Parameter Error（CC=5）→ 枚举失败。补丁后：EP0 的 Average TRB Length = 8，与 §6.2.3 控制端点建议一致，虚拟 xHC 枚举通过；真实硬件行为不变。tx_info 是 Endpoint Context 里承载 Average TRB Length 的字组，该值供带宽计算使用；而 Address Device 命令的参数正是指向这份 Input Context（§4.3.3 p76：「Issue an Address Device Command…where the command points to the Input Context data structure」）——与代码摘录（drafts/code-excerpts.json dev 组 L4404/L4412/L4416 的编址路径）对读，补丁改的就是命令带上报给 xHC 的上下文内容。验收口径：正文引用一律主线上游 hash f72b9aa821a2，本地另以 stable 回移件 dabf502ee893 做同内容 diff 核验——两树基线行号不同，新增行逐字相同。

上游 commit c1c8550e70401159184130a1afc6261db01fc0ce · 2026-03-05 · Zilin Guan（东南大学，LLVM 静态分析） · drivers/usb/host/xhci.c 1 文件 +2/-2 · hunks @@ -4146,7 +4146,7 @@ / @@ -4154,7 +4154,7 @@（xhci_disable_slot 两条错误路径）

完整补丁 · git show 逐字

commit c1c8550e70401159184130a1afc6261db01fc0ce
Author: Zilin Guan <zilin@seu.edu.cn>
Date:   Thu Mar 5 00:36:37 2026 +0200

    usb: xhci: Fix memory leak in xhci_disable_slot()
    
    xhci_alloc_command() allocates a command structure and, when the
    second argument is true, also allocates a completion structure.
    Currently, the error handling path in xhci_disable_slot() only frees
    the command structure using kfree(), causing the completion structure
    to leak.
    
    Use xhci_free_command() instead of kfree(). xhci_free_command() correctly
    frees both the command structure and the associated completion structure.
    Since the command structure is allocated with zero-initialization,
    command->in_ctx is NULL and will not be erroneously freed by
    xhci_free_command().
    
    This bug was found using an experimental static analysis tool we are
    developing. The tool is based on the LLVM framework and is specifically
    designed to detect memory management issues. It is currently under
    active development and not yet publicly available, but we plan to
    open-source it after our research is published.
    
    The bug was originally detected on v6.13-rc1 using our static analysis
    tool, and we have verified that the issue persists in the latest mainline
    kernel.
    
    We performed build testing on x86_64 with allyesconfig using GCC=11.4.0.
    Since triggering these error paths in xhci_disable_slot() requires specific
    hardware conditions or abnormal state, we were unable to construct a test
    case to reliably trigger these specific error paths at runtime.
    
    Fixes: 7faac1953ed1 ("xhci: avoid race between disable slot command and host runtime suspend")
    CC: stable@vger.kernel.org
    Signed-off-by: Zilin Guan <zilin@seu.edu.cn>
    Signed-off-by: Mathias Nyman <mathias.nyman@linux.intel.com>
    Link: https://patch.msgid.link/20260304223639.3882398-2-mathias.nyman@linux.intel.com
    Signed-off-by: Greg Kroah-Hartman <gregkh@linuxfoundation.org>

背景（何时 / 何硬件 / 谁报）

2026 年 3 月，东南大学 Zilin Guan 以基于 LLVM 框架的实验性静态分析工具发现并提交（工具随论文研发中，bug 最初在 v6.13-rc1 检出、主线仍在）；x86_64 allyesconfig、GCC 11.4.0 构建验证。这两条错误路径要求 host 已死或命令入环失败等异常状态才走到，难以构造运行时用例稳定触发，属「静态分析发现 + 构建验证」类交付。对应 §4.4 拔除链的下半程：设备拔除（PORTSC CCS 清 0 / CSC 置 1，p81）→ 端口变化事件 → usbcore 调 xhci_free_dev → xhci_disable_slot——控制器异常时走的正是被修的两条错误返回路径。

要解决的问题（症状 + 机制）

症状：xhci_disable_slot() 的两条错误路径（host 控制器已死 / Disable Slot 命令入环失败）各泄漏一个 completion 结构。机制：L4151 的 xhci_alloc_command(xhci, true, GFP_KERNEL) 第二参为 true 时，除命令结构外还分配 completion 结构；错误路径只用 kfree(command) 释放命令结构本身，completion 结构泄漏。规范背景（§4.4 p81）：检出拔除后软件须对受影响槽发 Disable Slot Command——若控制器恰在此刻死亡，命令发出前就返回，每次异常拔除都留下一个孤儿 completion。

代码逻辑（补丁前 → 后行为对照）

把两条错误路径的 kfree(command) 改为 xhci_free_command(xhci, command)（+2/-2）：后者同时释放命令结构与关联的 completion 结构；命令结构零初始化使 command->in_ctx 为 NULL，不会被误释放。补丁前：kfree 只放一半，completion 泄漏；补丁后：错误路径与成功路径（L4183）释放形态统一，任何退出路径都不留孤儿。与贴码对读：det:1 分配行（true=带 completion）、det:5 热行即被改的错误路径之一——本补丁已在基线 d9008e061539 内，贴码即补丁后形态；§4.4 拔除剧本的「异常退出」分支从此与正常分支同构。

补丁分帧动画 · 四幕走读 → 背景现场 → 补丁前错误行为 → diff 逐行走读 → 补丁后行为（对照规范出处）

969int xhci_alloc_virt_device(struct xhci_hcd *xhci, int slot_id,


970		struct usb_device *udev, gfp_t flags)


971{


972	struct xhci_virt_device *dev;


973	int i;


974


975	/* Slot ID 0 is reserved */


976	if (slot_id == 0 || xhci->devs[slot_id]) {


977		xhci_warn(xhci, "Bad Slot ID %d\n", slot_id);


978		return 0;


979	}


980

982	if (!dev)


983		return 0;


984


985	dev->slot_id = slot_id;


986


987	/* Allocate the (output) device context that will be used in the HC. */


988	dev->out_ctx = xhci_alloc_container_ctx(xhci, XHCI_CTX_TYPE_DEVICE, flags);


989	if (!dev->out_ctx)


990		goto fail;


991

993

995	dev->in_ctx = xhci_alloc_container_ctx(xhci, XHCI_CTX_TYPE_INPUT, flags);


996	if (!dev->in_ctx)


997		goto fail;


998


999	xhci_dbg(xhci, "Slot %d input ctx = %pad (dma)\n", slot_id, &dev->in_ctx->dma);


1000


1001	/* Initialize the cancellation and bandwidth list for each ep */


1002	for (i = 0; i < 31; i++) {


1003		dev->eps[i].ep_index = i;


1004		dev->eps[i].vdev = dev;


1005		INIT_LIST_HEAD(&dev->eps[i].cancelled_td_list);


1006		INIT_LIST_HEAD(&dev->eps[i].bw_endpoint_list);


1007	}


1008


1009	/* Allocate endpoint 0 ring */


1010	dev->eps[0].ring = xhci_ring_alloc(xhci, 2, TYPE_CTRL, 0, flags);


1011	if (!dev->eps[0].ring)


1012		goto fail;


1013


1014	xhci_ring_init(xhci, dev->eps[0].ring);


1015

1017

1019	xhci->dcbaa.ctx_array[slot_id] = cpu_to_le64(dev->out_ctx->dma);

1021		 slot_id,


1022		 &xhci->dcbaa.ctx_array[slot_id],


1023		 le64_to_cpu(xhci->dcbaa.ctx_array[slot_id]));

1026

1028

1034	if (dev->out_ctx)

commit 2eb03376151bb8585caa23ed2673583107bb5193
Author: Weitao Wang <WeitaoWang-oc@zhaoxin.com>
Date:   Tue Aug 19 15:58:43 2025 +0300

    usb: xhci: Fix slot_id resource race conflict
    
    xHC controller may immediately reuse a slot_id after it's disabled,
    giving it to a new enumerating device before the xhci driver freed
    all resources related to the disabled device.
    
    In such a scenario, device-A with slot_id equal to 1 is disconnecting
    while device-B is enumerating, device-B will fail to enumerate in the
    follow sequence.
    
    1.[device-A] send disable slot command
    2.[device-B] send enable slot command
    3.[device-A] disable slot command completed and wakeup waiting thread
    4.[device-B] enable slot command completed with slot_id equal to 1 and
                 wakeup waiting thread
    5.[device-B] driver checks that slot_id is still in use (by device-A) in
                 xhci_alloc_virt_device, and fail to enumerate due to this
                 conflict
    6.[device-A] xhci->devs[slot_id] set to NULL in xhci_free_virt_device
    
    To fix driver's slot_id resources conflict, clear xhci->devs[slot_id] and
    xhci->dcbba->dev_context_ptrs[slot_id] pointers in the interrupt context
    when disable slot command completes successfully. Simultaneously, adjust
    function xhci_free_virt_device to accurately handle device release.
    
    [minor smatch warning and commit message fix -Mathias]
    
    Cc: stable@vger.kernel.org
    Fixes: 7faac1953ed1 ("xhci: avoid race between disable slot command and host runtime suspend")
    Signed-off-by: Weitao Wang <WeitaoWang-oc@zhaoxin.com>
    Signed-off-by: Mathias Nyman <mathias.nyman@linux.intel.com>
    Link: https://lore.kernel.org/r/20250819125844.2042452-2-mathias.nyman@linux.intel.com
    Signed-off-by: Greg Kroah-Hartman <gregkh@linuxfoundation.org>

背景（何时 / 何硬件 / 谁报）

2025 年 8 月，兆芯（Zhaoxin）Weitao Wang 报告并修复；Fixes 追溯 7faac1953ed1（disable slot 与运行时挂起竞态修复引入的形态）。场景：device-A（slot_id=1）正在断开、device-B 正在枚举——xHC 对「同一 slot_id 先 disable 再 enable」的复用没有软件可见的延迟承诺，控制器可以在 disable 完成的瞬间就把刚释放的 slot_id 分给新设备。

要解决的问题（症状 + 机制）

六步竞态时序（补丁信息原文）：① A 发 Disable Slot；② B 发 Enable Slot；③ A 的 Disable 完成并唤醒等待线程；④ B 的 Enable 完成且拿到的正是 slot_id=1；⑤ B 在 xhci_alloc_virt_device 检查 xhci->devs[1] 仍被 A 占用 → 枚举失败；⑥ A 的 xhci_free_virt_device 才把 devs[1] 置 NULL——永远晚一步。规范背景：Table 4-1（p86）Disabled 态 DCBAA 指针无效、槽不可用——驱动侧软件镜像（devs[] / DCBAA）必须与 xHC 的槽状态同步翻转，而原实现把它们留到进程上下文的慢路径。

代码逻辑（补丁前 → 后行为对照）

把清理前移到命令完成事件的中断上下文：xhci_handle_cmd_disable_slot 增加 cmd_comp_code 形参（TRB_DISABLE_SLOT case 分派行同步传入），COMP_SUCCESS 时即刻清 xhci->dcbaa->dev_context_ptrs[slot_id] 与 xhci->devs[slot_id]——DCBAA 指针清零即 Disabled 态的规范形态，此后 B 的 Enable Slot 完成时 devs[1] 已空，枚举照常。配套：xhci_free_virt_device 改为显式传 virt_dev 指针、只在数组仍指向本设备时清指针（防误清新主）；新增 xhci_disable_and_free_slot() 收拢全部「禁槽 + 释放」调用对。与贴码对读：§4.4 段 det:6 热行即本补丁在中断上下文清 DCBAA 指针的新增块（基线形态 xhci->dcbaa.ctx_array[slot_id] = 0）、§4.5 段 slot:8 冲突检测热行（B 被挡的判定点）与 slot:9 DCBAA 装载热行（装载/清零互为正反操作）——本补丁已在基线 d9008e061539 内，贴码即补丁后形态。

补丁分帧动画 · 四幕走读 → 背景现场 → 补丁前错误行为 → diff 逐行走读 → 补丁后行为（对照规范出处）

§4.6Command Interface 命令接口 18 小节逐支短片线 · 机制片+十七命令+三支补丁片=18 小节全上线（批 A-D）

①大区导语 · 4.6.1 机制 + 17 支真命令

4.6 是命令集总章：4.6.1 Command Ring Operation 是后 17 支命令的公共机制地基（非命令），4.6.2-4.6.18 共 17 支真命令（其中六支可选规范项）。每小节一支短片 1:1 映射（R10 拍板：无总览、无合并、无命令族分组）；卡头类型标签＝导航视觉分区，不是视频分组。

The command interface of the xHC is managed through the Command Ring Control Register (CRCR). The CRCR Command Ring Pointer field provides a pointer to the Command Ring. Software places commands on the Command Ring, then rings the Host Controller Doorbell Register to notify the xHC. The xHC processes the commands and generates Command Completion Events on the Primary Event Ring to notify software of their completion status. This section describes the operation of the Command Ring and each of the
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L3-L9（p90）
Note: Software shall be responsible for all command timeouts. If a command times out, software may abort the command using the mechanism described in section 4.6.1.2.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L31-L33（p90）
模块六 · 命令受理台 → 单文件自包含 · Zone A 受理剧本（5 代表命令拼 TRB→入环→回执）· Zone B 命令×槽状态合法性矩阵（错配引原文拦截）

补丁片（选做）：三支已落地——ch4606p（d6759133e981 Configure Endpoint Context Entries 重算）· ch4609p（1174d44906d5 Stop EP 完成竞态重发）· ch4610p（0d9f78a92ef5 背靠背 Set TR Deq Ptr 越段挂死，修复函数已退役故无对读段）；准入判据=R10-SPEC §6 三判据（真实主线修复 / 三幕 ≤700f 可讲清 / 与该命令规范机制直接相关），无可准入者不硬凑。可选族七支无补丁片——无内核修复故事可配（R10-SPEC §6 判据）。

4.6.1Command Ring Operation机制p91-p93

规范引文（机制主线）

The Command Ring is a dedicated TRB Ring (refer to section 4.9 for a description of TRB Ring operation), which only allows those TRB types defined in Table 6-91. Only one Command Ring exists per xHC instance. System software is the producer of all Command TRBs and the xHC is the consumer.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L42-L46（p91）
Commands are issued by software to the xHC by: 1. Placing one or more Command Descriptors on the Command Ring and 2. Ringing the Host Controller Doorbell.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L63-L67（p91）
The xHC shall generate a Command Completion Event for every command. The Command TRB Pointer field of the Command Completion Event shall point to the Command TRB that initiated the event. The Completion Code field of the Command Completion Event shall indicate the completion status of the command. The Slot ID and VF ID fields shall reflect the values of the respective fields of the Command TRB that initiated the event. The Primary Event Ring receives all Command Completion Events.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L76-L82（p91）

drivers/usb/host/xhci-ring.c:1732-1809 · xhci_handle_command_timeout · d9008e061539
1732void xhci_handle_command_timeout(struct work_struct *work)


1733{


1734	struct xhci_hcd	*xhci;


1735	unsigned long	flags;


1736	char		str[XHCI_MSG_MAX];


1737	u64		hw_ring_state;


1738	u32		cmd_field3;


1739	u32		usbsts;


1740


1741	xhci = container_of(to_delayed_work(work), struct xhci_hcd, cmd_timer);


1742


1743	spin_lock_irqsave(&xhci->lock, flags);


1744


1745	/*


1746	 * If timeout work is pending, or current_cmd is NULL, it means we


1747	 * raced with command completion. Command is handled so just return.


1748	 */


1749	if (!xhci->current_cmd || delayed_work_pending(&xhci->cmd_timer)) {


1750		spin_unlock_irqrestore(&xhci->lock, flags);


1751		return;


1752	}


1753


1754	cmd_field3 = le32_to_cpu(xhci->current_cmd->command_trb->generic.field[3]);


1755	usbsts = readl(&xhci->op_regs->status);


1756	xhci_dbg(xhci, "Command timeout, USBSTS:%s\n", xhci_decode_usbsts(str, usbsts));


1757


1758	/* Bail out and tear down xhci if a stop endpoint command failed */


1759	if (TRB_FIELD_TO_TYPE(cmd_field3) == TRB_STOP_RING) {


1760		struct xhci_virt_ep	*ep;


1761


1762		xhci_warn(xhci, "xHCI host not responding to stop endpoint command\n");


1763


1764		ep = xhci_get_virt_ep(xhci, TRB_TO_SLOT_ID(cmd_field3),


1765				      TRB_TO_EP_INDEX(cmd_field3));


1766		if (ep)


1767			ep->ep_state &= ~EP_STOP_CMD_PENDING;


1768


1769		xhci_halt(xhci);


1770		xhci_hc_died(xhci);


1771		goto time_out_completed;


1772	}


1773


1774	/* mark this command to be cancelled */


1775	xhci->current_cmd->status = COMP_COMMAND_ABORTED;


1776


1777	/* Make sure command ring is running before aborting it */


1778	hw_ring_state = xhci_read_64(xhci, &xhci->op_regs->cmd_ring);


1779	if (hw_ring_state == ~(u64)0) {


1780		xhci_hc_died(xhci);


1781		goto time_out_completed;


1782	}


1783


1784	if ((xhci->cmd_ring_state & CMD_RING_STATE_RUNNING) &&


1785	    (hw_ring_state & CMD_RING_RUNNING))  {


1786		/* Prevent new doorbell, and start command abort */


1787		xhci->cmd_ring_state = CMD_RING_STATE_ABORTED;


1788		xhci_dbg(xhci, "Command timeout\n");


1789		xhci_abort_cmd_ring(xhci, flags);


1790		goto time_out_completed;


1791	}


1792


1793	/* host removed. Bail out */


1794	if (xhci->xhc_state & XHCI_STATE_REMOVING) {


1795		xhci_dbg(xhci, "host removed, ring start fail?\n");


1796		xhci_cleanup_command_queue(xhci);


1797


1798		goto time_out_completed;


1799	}


1800


1801	/* command timeout on stopped ring, ring can't be aborted */


1802	xhci_dbg(xhci, "Command timeout on stopped ring\n");


1803	xhci_handle_stopped_cmd_ring(xhci, xhci->current_cmd);


1804


1805time_out_completed:


1806	spin_unlock_irqrestore(&xhci->lock, flags);


1807	return;


1808}

System software may stop the execution of commands on the Command Ring by writing a ‘1’ to the Command Stop (CS) bit of the Command Ring Control register. Writing a ‘1’ to the CS bit shall stop the xHC from fetching additional CDs after the currently executing command completes, “stopping” the Command Ring. After the Command Ring has been successfully stopped, a
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L99-L103（p92）
流程图 · 4.6.1.2 中止命令（Aborting a Command） · 依据 spec-46.txt L121-L136
软件写 Command Ring Control 的 Command Abort（CA）=‘1’，中止当前命令
↓
当前有命令正在执行？
是
为被中止命令生成 Command Completion Event，Completion Code=Command Aborted
↓
Command Ring Dequeue Pointer 前进到下一 Command TRB
否
—
↓
生成 Command Completion Event：CC=Command Ring Stopped，Command TRB Pointer=当前 Dequeue Pointer
规范原文（本流程图依据 · 逐字机检）
System software may abort the execution of the current command by writing a ‘1’ to the Command Abort (CA) bit of the Command Ring Control register. Aborting a command on the Command Ring shall perform the following operations: • If a command is currently executing: o A Command Completion Event shall be generated for the aborted command with its Completion Code set to Command Aborted. o Advance the Command Ring Dequeue Pointer to point to the next Command TRB. • Generate a Command Completion Event with the Completion Code set to Command Ring Stopped and the Command TRB Pointer set to the current value of the Command Ring Dequeue Pointer.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L121-L136（p92）

The No Op command can be issued by software to exercise the TRB Ring mechanism of the xHC without affecting any xHC or USB Device state, or to report the current value of the Command Ring Dequeue Pointer.

4.6.3Enable Slot核心命令p94-p96

规范引文（机制主线）

The Enable Slot Command is issued by software to obtain an available Device Slot and to transition a Device Slot from the Disabled to the Enabled state.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L216-L217（p94）
When an Enable Slot Command is processed by the xHC, it will look for an available Device Slot. If a slot is available, the ID of a selected slot will be returned in the Slot ID field of a successful Command Completion Event on the Event Ring. If a Device Slot is not available, the Slot ID field shall be cleared to ‘0’ and a No Slots Available Error shall be returned in the Command Completion Event.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L220-L225（p94）
Upon the successful completion of an Enable Slot Command, system software shall use the Slot ID to link a Device Context data structure to the slot by writing a pointer to the Device Context in the Device Context Base Address Array[Slot ID] location. Undefined operation may occur if the Context Base Address Array entry is not updated prior to issuing a Command for the slot, or ringing the Default Control Endpoint (0) doorbell.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L226-L231（p94）
The algorithm for Device Slot ID selection is xHC implementation dependent.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L296-L296（p95）

When an Enable Slot Command is executed by the xHC it shall perform the following operations: • Insert a Command Completion Event on the Event Ring and initialize the following fields: o TRB Type = Command Completion Event (refer to Table 6-91). o Command TRB Pointer = The address of the Enable Slot Command TRB. o Determine if a Device Slot is available. o If a Device Slot is available: • Slot ID = ID of the selected Device Slot. • Completion Code = Success (refer to Table 6-90). • else // Device Slot is not available o Slot ID = ‘0’. o Completion Code = No Slots Available. • Clear all other fields of the event TRB to ‘0’. • Cycle bit = Event Ring’s PCS flag.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L268-L295（p95）
4.6.4Disable Slot核心命令p96-p97

规范引文（机制主线）

The Disable Slot Command is issued by software to force a Device Slot to the Disabled state. A typical use would be to free a Device Slot when a USB device is disconnected.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L308-L310（p96）
When a Disable Slot Command is processed by the xHC it shall: • Disable the Doorbell register for the slot • Free any bandwidth allocated to the periodic endpoints of the device • Terminate any slot related USB activity (for example, packet transfers) • Free any internal resources associated with the slot • Internally flag the slot as “available” for subsequent reassignment by an Enable Slot Command. that is, the Device Context Base Address Array entry for the slot is no longer considered valid by the xHC and software can free the Device Context, Transfer Ring, Stream Context Array, etc. data structures associated with the slot.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L311-L325（p96）
Note: Before software issues a Disable Slot Command the following conditions shall be true, otherwise undefined behavior may occur: • Any active endpoints associated with the slot shall be in the Stopped state or Idle in the Running state, and any outstanding Transfer Events shall have been received. • Any commands targeted at the slot that is being disabled shall be complete, that is, any outstanding Command Completion Events for the slot have been received.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L330-L339（p96）
Note: After software receives the Command Completion TRB for a Disable Slot Command it shall clear the respective DCBAA entry to ‘0’. This action allows the xHC to identify valid vs. invalid Device Slots after a Restore State operation.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L392-L395（p97）

drivers/usb/host/xhci-mem.c:869-926 · xhci_free_virt_device · d9008e061539
869void xhci_free_virt_device(struct xhci_hcd *xhci, struct xhci_virt_device *dev,


870		int slot_id)


871{


872	int i;


873	int old_active_eps = 0;


874


875	/* Slot ID 0 is reserved */


876	if (slot_id == 0 || !dev)


877		return;


878


879	/* If device ctx array still points to _this_ device, clear it */


880	if (dev->out_ctx &&


881	    xhci->dcbaa.ctx_array[slot_id] == cpu_to_le64(dev->out_ctx->dma))


882		xhci->dcbaa.ctx_array[slot_id] = 0;


883


884	trace_xhci_free_virt_device(dev);


885


886	if (dev->tt_info)


887		old_active_eps = dev->tt_info->active_eps;


888


889	for (i = 0; i < 31; i++) {


890		if (dev->eps[i].ring)


891			xhci_ring_free(xhci, dev->eps[i].ring);


892		if (dev->eps[i].stream_info)


893			xhci_free_stream_info(xhci,


894					dev->eps[i].stream_info);


895		/*


896		 * Endpoints are normally deleted from the bandwidth list when


897		 * endpoints are dropped, before device is freed.


898		 * If host is dying or being removed then endpoints aren't


899		 * dropped cleanly, so delete the endpoint from list here.


900		 * Only applicable for hosts with software bandwidth checking.


901		 */


902


903		if (!list_empty(&dev->eps[i].bw_endpoint_list)) {


904			list_del_init(&dev->eps[i].bw_endpoint_list);


905			xhci_dbg(xhci, "Slot %u endpoint %u not removed from BW list!\n",


906				 slot_id, i);


907		}


908	}


909	/* If this is a hub, free the TT(s) from the TT list */


910	xhci_free_tt_info(xhci, dev, slot_id);


911	/* If necessary, update the number of active TTs on this root port */


912	xhci_update_tt_active_eps(xhci, dev, old_active_eps);


913


914	if (dev->in_ctx)


915		xhci_free_container_ctx(xhci, dev->in_ctx);


916	if (dev->out_ctx)


917		xhci_free_container_ctx(xhci, dev->out_ctx);


918


919	if (dev->udev && dev->udev->slot_id)


920		dev->udev->slot_id = 0;


921	if (dev->rhub_port && dev->rhub_port->slot_id == slot_id)


922		dev->rhub_port->slot_id = 0;


923	if (xhci->devs[slot_id] == dev)


924		xhci->devs[slot_id] = NULL;


925	kfree(dev);


926}

4.6.5Address Device核心命令p97-p102

规范引文（机制主线）

The Address Device Command is issued by software to transition a Device Slot from the Enabled to the Default or Addressed state or from the Default to the Addressed state, depending on the state of the Block Set Address Request (BSR) flag.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L400-L403（p97）
When an Address Device Command is processed by the xHC it shall enable the device’s Default Control Endpoint, select an address for the USB device, and issue a USB SET_ADDRESS request to the USB Device. The SET_ADDRESS request for a USB2 device shall be issued to Address ‘0’. The SET_ADDRESS request for a USB3 device shall be issued using the Route String.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L404-L408（p97）
Note: If the Block Set Address Request (BSR) flag is ‘0’ in the Address Device Command TRB, then the xHC shall select a USB Device Address and issue a SET_ADDRESS request to a USB device as part of an Address Device Command. If the Block Set Address Request (BSR) flag is ‘1’ then the xHC shall not issue a SET_ADDRESS request to a USB device as part of an Address Device Command. In either case, all other operations described in this section for the Address Device Command are performed. The BSR flag may be used by software to provide compatibility with legacy USB devices which require their Device Descriptor to be read before receiving a SET_ADDRESS request.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L437-L445（p98）
The Add Context flags A0 and A1 of the Input Control Context data structure (in the Input Context) shall be set to ‘1’, and all remaining Add Context and Drop Context flags shall all be cleared to ‘0’.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L456-L458（p98）

4387	 * If this is the first Set Address since device plug-in or


4388	 * virt_device realloaction after a resume with an xHCI power loss,


4389	 * then set up the slot context.


4390	 */


4391	if (!slot_ctx->dev_info)


4392		xhci_setup_addressable_virt_dev(xhci, udev);

When an Address Device Command is executed by the xHC it shall perform the following operations: • Insert a Command Completion Event on the Event Ring and initialize the following fields: o TRB Type = Command Completion Event (refer to Table 6-91). o Command TRB Pointer = The address of the Address Device Command TRB. o Slot ID = The value of the command’s Slot ID. o If the Device Slot identified by the command’s Slot ID field has been previously enabled by an Enable Slot Command: • Retrieve the pointer to the Output Device Context of the selected Device Slot. • If the Block Set Address Request (BSR) flag = ‘1’ • If the slot is in the Enabled state: • Copy all fields of the Input Slot Context to the Output Slot Context. • Copy all fields of the Input Endpoint 0 Context to the Output Endpoint 0 Context. • Set the Endpoint State (EP State) field in the Output Endpoint 0
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L505-L533（p99）

4.6.6Configure Endpoint核心命令p102-p112

规范引文（机制主线）

The Configure Endpoint Command is issued by software to enable, disable, or reconfigure endpoints associated with a target configuration.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L659-L660（p102）
Configuring a device. To set a configuration in a device, software shall issue a Configure Endpoint Command to the xHC in conjunction with issuing USB SET_CONFIGURATION request to the device. This command shall be used to enable the set of Device Slot endpoints selected by the target configuration, and transition a Device Slot from the Addressed to the Configured state. Undefined behavior may occur if TDs are posted for endpoints enabled by this command and the SET_CONFIGURATION request associated with this command is not successfully completed by the USB device.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L667-L675（p102）
Note: Setting the Deconfigure (DC) flag to ‘1’ in the Configure Endpoint Command TRB is equivalent to setting Input Context Drop Context flags 2-31 to ‘1’ and Add Context 2-31 flags to ‘0’. If the DC flag = ‘1’, the Input Context Pointer field shall be ignored by the xHC and the Output Slot Context Context Entries field shall be set to ‘1’.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L689-L693（p102）
xHC behavior is undefined if the Drop Context (D) flag is ‘0’, the Add Context (A) flag is ‘1’, and the Output Endpoint Context is not in the Disabled state (that is, software is trying to add an endpoint without dropping its current resources).
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L856-L859（p105）

规范算法全文收纳（算法流程图 + 折叠原文逐字备查；短片降密度：片内只讲机制主线）

The following rules apply to processing a Configure Endpoint Command: • The xHC resources assigned to a Device Slot are not modified until after all Drop Context and Add Context flags are evaluated. • The Slot State field of a Device Slot Context is not modified until after all Drop Context and Add Context flags are evaluated. • The xHC maintains a global Resources Available variable, which is initialized to indicate all xHC resources are available. A Resource is an xHC implementation defined metric, which refers to the internal xHC data structures, buffer space, or other implementation specific resources required to support an endpoint type.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L750-L763（p103）

上游 commit d6759133e9815ef807b17dc752aff8c3771b7444 · 2014-06-24 · Julius Werner（Chromium） · drivers/usb/host/xhci.c 1 文件 +18/-33 · hunks @@ -1582,12 / @@ -1634,24 / @@ -1674,11 / @@ -1693,7 / @@ -1759,24 / @@ -2746,8

完整补丁 · git show 逐字

commit d6759133e9815ef807b17dc752aff8c3771b7444
Author: Julius Werner <jwerner@chromium.org>
Date:   Tue Jun 24 17:14:42 2014 +0300

    usb: xhci: Correct last context entry calculation for Configure Endpoint
    
    The current XHCI driver recalculates the Context Entries field in the
    Slot Context on every add_endpoint() and drop_endpoint() call. In the
    case of drop_endpoint(), it seems to assume that the add_flags will
    always contain every endpoint for the new configuration, which is not
    necessarily correct if you don't make assumptions about how the USB core
    uses the add_endpoint/drop_endpoint interface (add_flags only contains
    endpoints that are new additions in the new configuration).
    
    Furthermore, EP0_FLAG is not consistently set in add_flags throughout
    the lifetime of a device. This means that when all endpoints are
    dropped, the Context Entries field can be set to 0 (which is invalid and
    may cause a Parameter Error) or -1 (which is interpreted as 31 and
    causes the driver to keep using the old, incorrect value).
    
    The only surefire way to set this field right is to also take all
    existing endpoints into account, and to force the value to 1 (meaning
    only EP0 is active) if no other endpoint is found. This patch implements
    that as a single step in the final check_bandwidth() call and removes
    the intermediary calculations from add_endpoint() and drop_endpoint().
    
    Signed-off-by: Julius Werner <jwerner@chromium.org>
    Signed-off-by: Mathias Nyman <mathias.nyman@linux.intel.com>
    Signed-off-by: Greg Kroah-Hartman <gregkh@linuxfoundation.org>

背景（何时 / 谁修 / 为什么是它）

2014 年 6 月，Chromium OS 的 Julius Werner 提交、Mathias Nyman 与 Greg Kroah-Hartman 签收。Context Entries 是 Slot Context 里「本槽最后一个有效端点上下文的索引」——§4.6.6 执行算法（p110）规定每次 Configure Endpoint 后由 xHC 收束：全 Disabled 落 '1'，否则落最后有效索引；p111 还规定Add 旗标指到的 DCI 超过该值即 Parameter Error。这个字段的内核侧计算，从环扩张时代起就散落在xhci_add_endpoint()/xhci_drop_endpoint() 里增量维护。

要解决的问题（症状 + 机制）

症状：某些「全部端点被 drop」的配置切换（如切到 Alternate Interface 0）后，Configure Endpoint 命令被 xHC 以 Parameter Error 拒绝，或驱动继续沿用旧值。机制（message 原文）：drop_endpoint() 的增量计算「似乎假定 add_flags 总含新配置的全部端点」——实际 add_flags 只含新配置里新增的端点；且 EP0_FLAG 在设备生命周期里并非恒置。两个假设任一不成立，全 drop 时 Context Entries 就被算成 0（非法值——p111 Parameter Error 判据「Add 的 DCI > Context Entries」直接命中）或 -1（按 LAST_CTX 宏解读为 31，导致继续使用旧的错误值）。

代码逻辑（补丁前 → 后行为对照）

把两处增量维护整块删除（add/drop 各一块），改为 xhci_check_bandwidth() 发命令前的终点一步重算：i 自 31 下探，取第一个「ring 存活且未被 Drop」或「Add 旗标置位」的端点写 LAST_CTX(i)；全不命中则 i==1 兜底——LAST_CTX(1) 即「只剩 EP0」。补丁前：每步增量的两处假设任一不成立就写坏值；补丁后：终点一次算清，且 i==1 分支正是规范 p110「全 Disabled → Context Entries=\'1\'」的内核直译。与贴码对读：下方补丁对读段琥珀高亮行（Fix up 注释行）即修复循环所在——本补丁已在基线 d9008e061539 内，贴码即补丁后形态；分帧走读见 ch4606p（scene=28）。

补丁对读段 commit d9008e061539 · 修复后形态（基线即含此补丁）· 琥珀高亮行 = 修复循环所在

4.6.7Evaluate Context核心命令p112-p114

规范引文（机制主线）

Evaluate Context The Evaluate Context Command is issued by software to inform the xHC that specific fields in the Device Context data structures have been modified. There are several cases where parameters associated with a Slot Context or the Default Control Endpoint Context are initially unknown, which shall be updated after the slot has entered the Addressed state. for example, the Max Packet Size of the control endpoint may be determined only after software reads the Device Descriptor from the device through the control endpoint. The Device
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L1296-L1303（p112）
Descriptor shall be read to determine whether a device is a hub or not, etc. When an Evaluate Context Command is processed by the xHC it shall only affect the parameters identified by the respective context. Refer to the Evaluate Context Command Usage sub-sections in section 4.5.2 and 6.2.2.3 for
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L1304-L1307（p112）
If the Slot State is Disabled when an Evaluate Context Command is received, the xHC shall generate a Slot Not Enabled Error Event on the Event Ring. Upon successful completion of an Evaluate Context Command, the xHC shall begin executing with the updated context parameters.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L1314-L1317（p112）
The xHC shall consider an Endpoint Context invalid if the DCI of an Add Context flag = ‘1’ is greater than the value of Context Entries.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L1417-L1418（p114）

流程图 · 4.6.7 Evaluate Context 发行算法（system software） · 依据 spec-46.txt L1326-L1332 + L1338-L1355
分配并初始化命令的 Input Context：Add Context flags 指示受影响 Context 的 ID；Drop Context flags 全部清 ‘0’
↓
在 Command Ring 插入 Evaluate Context Command：TRB Type=Evaluate Context（Table 6-91）；Slot ID=目标槽；Input Context Pointer=Input Context 基址；其余字段清 ‘0’；Cycle bit=命令环 PCS
↓
写 Host Controller Doorbell：DB Target=Host Controller Command
规范原文（本流程图依据 · 逐字机检）
To issue an Evaluate Context Command, system software shall perform the following operations: • Allocate and initialize an Input Context data structure for the command. • Insert an Evaluate Context Command on the Command Ring
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L1326-L1332（p112）
• TRB Type = Evaluate Context Command (refer to Table 6-91). • The Add Context flags shall be initialized to indicate the IDs of the Contexts affected by the command. Refer to sections 6.2.2.3 and 6.2.3.3 for the specific Context fields that shall be evaluated. • Set all Drop Context flags to ‘0’. • Slot ID = ID of the target Device Slot. • Input Context Pointer = The base address of the Input Context data structure. • Clear all other fields of the command TRB to ‘0’. • Cycle bit = Command Ring’s PCS flag. • Write the Host Controller Doorbell with DB Target = Host Controller Command.
—— evidence/spec-46.txt L1338-L1355（p113）
